*Page 1*


A garin Gumau, gari ne da fulani da manoma ?an gudun hijira da suka fito daga Jos suke zama, sun fito daga barikin Ladi da Yalwan Shandam.
Garin Gumai wani yanki ne da ya had'a yankoki guda biyu, Bauchi da Jos. Toh duk abinda aka shuka sai Allah ya albarkace shi. Manoman suna noma kabeji, gurji wato cumcumbe. Latass, karasa, dan kalin turawa da ta hausa, kai har doya sunayi, ga masara wanda aka zuwa da manyan j5 ana saya.

Mutanen gumau akwai ha?inkai da taimakon juna.

Wannan knn.....
Hajiya Tabawa wacce yan garin ke mata lak'abi da Yaddiko, tsohuwace mai zuciyar niman na kanta, gata bafulatana ce amma mai zuciyar nima, dukda Y'ay'anta masu ku?i ne, amma haka bai hanata niman nakanta ba, dan har noma take ga kiwon da take.
Ko maigari a gurinta yake niman aron ku?i dan ?an hasafi, duk wani mai ji da ku?i a garin gumau yasan bai kai Yaddiko ba, dan karamin ?anta babban ma'aikacene a custom, babban kuwa shima ?an kasuwa ne,dan har gidajen mai gare shi. Allah ya albarkaci Yaddiko da yara hu?u biyu maza biyu mata sai dai ?aya mace ta rasu.

Alhaji Imarana shine babba yana bauchi a Ibrahim bako yake da zama, Sai Alhaji Rabi'u yana zaune a abuja da iyalinshi shike karamin ?anta.
Sai Hajiya Sadika da take aure a jos, da yaranta, sai Hajiya Aisha wacce ta rasu a shekaran da Yaddiko ta tsinci Yar gidan Yaddiko, ta sanadin hatsrin mota ita ?aya ce aka tsinceta dan motar ta kama da wuta.

A duniya babu abinda Yaddiko ta tsana irin kukan Shatuwarta dan lokacin da aka sameta itace ta amsheta da saran ko danginta zasu tawo nimanta. Har yanzun shiru kake ji. Daukacin mutanen garin gumau suna shakkar jarabar yaddiko da yar gidanta, dan duk laifin da tayi hakuri suke aje gurin mai gari a gaya mishi.

Wannan knn bari mu duba yar gidan Yaddiko.

Faran faran kamar mi faret haka take tafiya tare da ?aga kuran hanyar, sanye take da unifoam ruwan bula. Tsabar niman tada hankalin jama'a take ?aga kuran.

A gaban Lamiyo mai kosae ta ci birki, kallon dandazon mutanen da suke bin layi tayi ta ta?e bakinta. Da mugun sauri tayi kan kaskon mangya?ar take duk sukayi baya.

"Yar gidan Y
addiko tinkiya sarkin tam?ele."

Murmushi tayi sabida tana nazarin abinda zata mishi ai tasan nufin maganarshi.

Washe hakorana nayi ina kallon Ila da taburin shayinshi, girgiza kai nayi na koma da baya, na gyara zaman hijabina wanda yake a cukurku?e. Sakamakon tauneshi da nake, idan duniya tayi min da?i.

Da gudu na antayo na dunbuli kosan tare da ture wasu mutanen suka ture taberin ila mai shayi, jin karan kwai kake tassssssss. Na cikawa bujena iska ina dariya,

"Ba burki burki."

Har zan wucce sai na hango gwaibar gidansu Ta asibi, kallon katangar gidan nayi na buga tsalle na kama jikin bangon na ?anne kamar wata yar biri na tsinko, dayawa sannan nace.
"Ta asibiii ki hito gani nan na sace miki gwaiba alkur'anan nayi maganin masu dukiyar ?an karuna."
Aikuwa yan gidan suka fito asaba'in na dire abuna na arta a guje duk na zubda gwaibar,

Ban tsaya ba sai makaranta sabida ranar monday ne. Ina shiga naga firifet suna tsaren lati, fuska nayi na shige," keee " inji garbebe, turusss nayi na tsaya tare da cin wani burki na juya, tare da kallonshi.
"Zo nan yar kwal uba kinhi sauran yaran ne."
Ta?e fuskan nayi tare da girgiza kai, nace.
"Ni ba yar kwal uba bace."

Na fa?i haka tare da murguda ?an mitsisin bakina.

Kaiiina sukayo, na tarta a guje gurin da ake asanbili na fa?a cikin ?alibai take na rusa musu jerin gwanonsu ta hanyar zubewa a kasa.

Shiru hedimansa yayi yana kallona, mikewa nayi ina kallon mutanen gurin har da wani bakon da mukayi a makarantar.

Sake bakina nayi ina kallonshi, cike da al'ajabi sabida siririntakar shi, da kuma muninshi.

"Hedimansa wannan kuma waye kuka jajju?o mana mkrnta salon muyi mafarki yo kaga kugun kaman ayiwa sauro zanzaro, ga manyan idanu kamar an watsawa bududdugi barkono a idanu sam bai min ba, gaskiya a sauya shi dan matukar na cigaba da gani hi, kuka zanyi."

Har aka gama aka sallamemu zuwa ajinmu, aji biyar nake.

Malam ayaga lutine ya shigo mana, kallonshi nayi tare da gintse dariyata na kalli katon cikinshi, ya cigaba da dariya a fili.
Cikin fusata yace.
"Shatuwa fito waje."
Fitowa nayi na tsaya kerere.
Kallona yayi yace.
"Mi yasaki dariya."
Nuna mishi tumbinshi nayi cikin dariya nace.
"Yaseen idan ka juya baya tamkar an sanyawa agwagwa buje, idan ka juyo tumbinka kamar tsohon kwaton da yagaji da duniya, sannan idan kana magana sai fuskarka tayi tankar kahin hainu."

Ya buga musu tsawa.

Komawa mazauni na nayi ina dariya haka ya gama lissafinshi ya fita.

Wiwi kamar wacce akace ga yaddiko zata shura. Mamaki ne ya kama yan ajin, shima malamin duk ya ru?e, yans tambayana.
"wai ayu kirayin."

Ji nayi kamar ya kuntuttuma min ashariya nasaka hannuwana a tsakar kaina na fasa ihu, tare da zaman yan bori. Take wani yaro ys fita ta taga ya sheka a guje sai ofishin hedimansa ya labarta mishi yana ganin hedimansa ya tawo ya fita da gudu daga makarantar sai gidan yaddiko.

Tana zaune ta kunna radeyonta sai murzawa) tana kokarin kamo gidan raidiyon bauchi. Da gudu yaron ya fa?a kanta yace.

Ai batasan lokacin da ta wacakalar da raidiyon ba ta finciko hijabinta, daga ita sai ?aurin kirji. Fayau fayau......

Ina zaune malamai sun kewayeni kai sai ka rantse wani gamo nayi.

Ina jin muryan sanyin idaniyata na kara fashewa da kuka, nace.

Da sauri ta isa inda nake tace.
"Na shiga uku, ni tabawa Yan nan fa?a min waye yaci mutunci a cikinsu."
Tana magana tare da watsar da hannunta.
Girgixa kai nayi cikin kuka nace.

"Na'am Yar nan." tace min duk ta kagu taji mi yasani kuka,

Cikin jimami ta yatsina fuska sannan tace.

Lallu?o ha?ar zaninta Yaddiko tayi cikin tsare gida ta ?an tari goronta ta jefa a bakinta, "kurrsu" ta taune goron sannan tace.
"Yo banda abinki ai da ?an kyalla da wancar mutumin kamar an zaga kara an karya ke dai banbancinsu wannan mutum ne ?an kyalla kuma ?an taure ne."

"Yo akwai wata cuta ce da ta cuce cutar barbara."

Nahiga uku yaddiko, dama barbaran akwai cutarhi ce?"

Juyawa tayi tabarni a gurin. Haka malamin yafita, bai koyar ba.

.......... Ana tashi tara na ?auki yar jakata da nabarta tun jumma'a na fita.

Gida na dawo na sauya kayana na sungumi tayana na fita da ita ina garata.

Mikewa tayi a fusace tayo kaina na arta a guje, sai majalinsa ?an izala. Birki naci daga nesa, na tsurawa wani bakon da sukayi ido. Kyakyawa dashi sai yar gemunshi yayi min kama da gemun.

Page...2
Kasake nayi ina kallon yanda mutumin yake magana, ?an gemunshi nan sai wani motsi yake, girgiza kaina nayi cikin jin da?i nace.
"Ahe a cikin mutane akwai masu kama da akuyoyi yo dubi gemun wancar kaman ta sahabi Allah sarki."

Tattara dukkanin karfina na lafta a guje, ga uban kuran da na tadda. Kallona malam Dalha yayi ya girgiza kai yace.
"Ina ga gidan Yaddiko kuke nima, ga can takadarirrayar jikanta can tana tadda kura."

Tunda naga ya nuno ni, nayi kwafa a raina nace.
"Nasan za'a rina."
A nutse na iso gurinsu na kalli malam dalha nace.
"Naga kana nuno ni? Ince dai lahiya? Da fatan ba wani hairrin za'a ?ulla min ba."

Kallona bakon yayi cike da mamaki, kardai Shatun Yaddiko ce haka.
"Ke tafi can shashasha, baki kukayi daga birni."
Kamar wacce aka ce min ga baki daga kasar waje na wani washe jajjayen hakorana nace.
"Yo kace baki mukayi daga binni, toh kardai a kintsa musu wani hairrin dan nasan a garin ga banda Yaddiko ba me kaunata duk masu kaunata dan ku?in Yaddiko suke kaunata, mutum yayi magana Kwarankwatsa idan yazo cin bahi nasa Yaddiko ta hana hi."

Bai ce komi ba, yace.
"Toh bawan Allah ga Yar gidan Yaddiko nan zata kaiku har gida."
Mika mishi hannu bakon yayi suka gaisa, ni kuwa na leka hannun cikin katsalanda nace.
"Karka wani bahi cin anci, kawai kazo muje."

Da mamaki ya kalle ni, ni kuwa sai wani bu?e hancina nake, irin na zama wata a garin gumau.
Cikin dadda?ar muryanshi yace.
"Toh kishiga motar mana."
Juyawa nayi na kalle shi sama da kasa, nace.
"Yaddiko ta hanani higa motar mutane dan tace yan han jini ne suke cewa a higa motarsu, kagaku biyo ni sannu ahankali zan kaiku Unguwar gabar."

Gaba nayi abuna shi kuma ya shige cikin motar, ya kalli abokin tafiyar yace.
"Man kaga yanda Shatun Yaddiko ta girma kuwa."
D'ago kanshi yayi ya kalli mai maganar sannan yace.
"Sai ya."
Dake yasan halin abokinshi bai kuma cewa komi ba yajasu suka bi bayana.

Wani irin tafiya ake mai cike da abin takaici da ban haushi kamar abin arziki na ?oye hali, muna cikin tafiya na hango. Audu ya sayo manja a leda, sai sauri yake zai kai a musu abinci nasha gabanshi nace.
"Yawwa nakama ka."
Zaro ido yaron yayi cikin tsoro yace.
"Shatuwa, kiyi hakuri Iyata ta aikeni nasayo manjan alale ne."
"Kai tafi can haihaihai, kawai sai na durje bakinka da ka zagen kace min tinkiya."

Hakuri ya shiga bani, na dalla mishi mari, kafin ya dawo hayacin shi na sami tsakiyar kanshi na zuba mishi rankwashi, sannan na ture shi manjan ya fashe.

Duk dukar dana mishi bai ji zafi ba kamar fashewar manjan na kuwa tsinka a guje, karshe fitowa sukayi a motar suka ?aga yaron, da yake kuka. Biyanshi ku?in manjan sukayi.

A can gaba kuwa, ina zuwa dandali naga talatule da tiren gya?a, Talle da kwanon dafaffiyar masara, xuwa nayi na tsaya musu nace.
"A bani alkoro ko nayi aikin."

Banza suka min nasake nanatawa basu kula ni, fakansu nayi na wafauce gya?a da masaran wanda yayi sanadin tintsirewar kayansu baki ?aya.

Koda na isa bakin kasuwa samu nayi ana dambe tsakanin. Hudu da Sanda. Sun yasar da kayansu na talle a gurin, dariya nayi nace.
"Mahaukata yaseen sai na ?auki, yar yau da kosan rokon."
Bu?e kayan nayi na ?iba, aikuwa suka yo kaina na falfala a guje,

Ban tsaya ba sai a gida, nasami bakin har sun iso, ko inda suke ban kalla ba na bu?e gidan na shige abuna.
Kai tsaye bishiyar maina na haye abuna, dan naji kamar Yaddiko bata gida.

A kofar gidan mai gari kuwa, takanas ya tura aka kira Yaddiko ta zauna.
Shiru fadan tayi, Mai gari yace.
"Yaddiko!!! Mutane suna kawo min karan jikanyarki fa, zan koreta a garina dan ta buwayi mutanen gari."

Shiru tayi sannan tace.
"Toh Yalla?ai ta'adin nawa tayi?"

Nan aka shiga mata lissafi bata damu ba, ta ciro ku?insu ta biya sannan ta mike cikin bala'i tace.
"Banda rashin mutunci har nice za'a kawo ni gaban Mai gari toh a kwaryan cin tuwonku dan sai kunzo cin bashi zan. Kankaro muku ?iban albarkan da kuka min, masu son ganin bayan shatuwa kuwa sunci kaya kurwata kurrrr."

Ka?e gyaleta tayi tabarsu a gurin, tayo gida.
.......A kofar gida ta ci birki tace.
"Kaiii Mutanen bauchi da Abuja barkanku da zuwa kushigo sannun Mahar da Najibu."

Tura kofar gidan tayi, duk suka shigo cikin gidan. Falonta takaisu, nan tashiga hidima dasu.
Ina sama abuna cikin kwanciyar hankali da nutsuwa nake barci a jikin reshen.

Fitowa tayi taga takalmina a kasar bishi, tace.
"Shatuwa, sauko kije gidan Mai tafasa ki amso min nayi dambu ace."

Leko kaina nayi nace.
"Toh Yaddiko."
Dirowa nayi na amshi naira hansin ?inta na fita, ina cikin tafiya muka ha?u da Sahura.

Kallonta nayi na kauda kaina dan ta cika gulma da tsirfa.
Da sauri tayo kaina tana washe hakora tace.
"Yawwa, Shatuwa dama ina nimanki, Da wata labari"

Harara na banka mata. Tare da ta?e bakina nace.
"Munahufa, yau kuma wata haihaihar ce tayi, magana a gabanki har kika zo gayya min."

Sosa kai tayi alamun jin kunya.
"Hmm dama Sa'aden gidan Tabayye ita min wai Yaddiko tsintarki tayi."
Ta?e baki nayi, ina ka?a kai nace.
"Hikenan dama."
"A'a wai ke baki da asali a titi aka tsinceki gobara ta cinye iyarki da Babanki."

Juyawa nayi cikin jin haushi nace.
"Wato Sa'aden da take zuwa cin hinkafa a gidanmu hine zata ci amanata, hikenan zan kamata dan kwal ubanta zata gaya min inda taji labarin ni ?in yar tsintuwa ce."

Juyawa nayi ban kara cewa komi ba, na dawo gida ina shiga Yaddiko tace.
"Shatu ina tafasar."
Kallonta nayi na ajiye mata ku?in na shige gurin dabobinta na zauna. Gaban uwar garke na fashe da kuka sosai.
Dabbobin dake sun san halina zuba min ido sukayi, jikin Yaddiko na rawa tayo garken cikin kuka nace.
"Kyau kinji wai nice yar tsintuwa, na higa uku na lalace. Wai iyata da babana wuta ta cinyesu. Kuma kunsan wacce ta fa?i haka kawata Sa'ade fa."

Aikuwa dabobbin suka ce min "Maaaaaaa"
Cikin yarda da abinda suka ce nace.
"Wato fansa ya tabbata ga maciya amana ko."
Aikuwa uwar garke tace min.
"Mahaaaa."
"Kaiii wankesu kin gama min, komi dole naci kwal ubanta."

Cike da mamaki Yaddiko tace.
"Wata yar wofi ce tace iyarki ta kone."
Tura mata baki nayi nace.
"Sa'aden tabayye mana, hi yasa ban sayo miki tafasar ba kawai ki girka musu hinkafa da..."

Rike bakina nayi nace.
"Ki kawo ku?i naje mahauta na sayo musu nama dan Yaseen baza'a ta?a min, kaji ko zabi ba, dan nayi musu alkawarin kare rayukarsu, kuma da zaran na bari aka yankasu tankar cin amana ce."
Gya?a min kai tayi sannan taciro min jaka guda ta bani tace.
"Gashi ta jaka guda."

Da sauri na sungumi tayata, na fita ina gara tayar.
Har naje nasayo namar na dawo kawai na ratsa ta maciya, da gudu na shige da tayata kan kayan miyar Nata'alla, tussss suka zubo tare da fashewa.
Kara kaimin guduna nayi naje naja rumfar salele take ya zube akanshi,
Kafin mai kifi ya ankara na jefa mishi tayar a kaskonshi, na arta a guje da laidar naman.
"Kutare mana yar kwal uba, kaii Yar gidan yaddiko kin addabemu"
Inji Nata'alla
A hanyar dawowa na gamu da Sa'ade ta bayanta na shakota nace.
"Dan kwal uwatai hine kikayi gulmata ke nan bazan samu masu fa?a min bane hegiya karamar munahuka, da hegen kananun idanu kamar na y'ayan tsak'a yau zanci mutuncinki yanda ya dace."

Kamar zata fashe da kuka. Yara sai ce min suke.
"Shatuwa!!!"
Aikuwa kaina yayi gingime. Bansan lokacin da na kifeta da mari, Cikin zubda kwalla tace.
"Ayya shatu kiyi hakuri Innata ce ba lahiya."

Ta?e baki nayi na tureta na kara gaba abuna.

Ina isa gidan mika mata sakon, sallamar mutane yasani lekawa naga mutanen da nayiwa ?arna a hanyar dawowa tace suka biyo ni, ta?e bakina nayi nace.

Tace bakinta na ?aki, Y'an gwabjejjen yafito sabida sallamar da ake, gani ni kuma na haye sama.

Fitowar ?an me gemun ya kalle shi alamun tambaya.

Ajiyar zuciya suka sauke cikin gida Ya shigo ya bu?e jakarshi ya fidda ku?i ya fita kofar gidan ya basu, akan su raba. Tsagal ina sama nace.

D'ago kai yayi ya tsura min ido ranshi na kara ?aci, zabga min harara yayi. Ni kuwa na murguda mishi baki.

Bai ce komi ba ya shige kallona mai gem? yayi aikuwa na sauko daga bishiyar na zauna a gaban Yaddiko na fashe da kuka.

Kallona duk sukayi cike da mamaki, kallona tayi a ki?ime tace.

Nuna mata mai gemun nayi nace.

Zaro ido tayi tace.
"Kuma haka ne fa, Kaii Najibu kaji wai kama kake da Dan taurena da aka sace dan shima gemu ne dashi kaman kai, gashi kyakyawa kamarka."

Amadadin yaji zafin maganar da nayi sai ya sake murmushi yace.
"Yaddiko kina fama da Shatu haka ta girma, dama."

"Eh wallahi tagirma shekara goma sha ?aya fa, dan ma bata da girman kashine. Ga bakin mutane dake damunta wallahi duk sun saka mata ido."

Dariya yayi mara sauti yace.
"Na lura mutane sun sanya mata ido, Shatu amma daga yau ba shatu zan cigaba da kiranki ba sai Ummuna Aisha, zaki bimu binni."
Matsawa jikin Yaddiko nayi ina matsar kwalla nace.
"Kwarankwatsa bani zuwa ko ina ko Yaddiko."

"Eh amma ai zamu, dan an kusan bikin Yar gidan Sadika." tace min.
Gya?a kaina nayi ina jan majina tare da tsotse nakan hancina, Murmushi yasake yi, a karo na uku. Mike ?ayan yayi yana yunkurin amai....

Tsaki nayi nace.
"Mai gemu miye sunan wancar."
"Suna na Ya Najib, shi kuma Ya Maheer, ba mai gemu ba."

Cikin ikon Allah, na haihu inda nasami Y'a mace.
Koda labarin haihuwar ya isa maiduguri, ba'a wani damu ba, sai dai Nanah ce tayi duk abinda ya dace aka kawo min,
Ta ?angaren Bamai shima yayi duk abinda ya dace.
Abubuwa dai sun faru masu da?i da marasa da?i, ana saura sati ?aya zamuyi arba'in Allah yayiwa mai martaba rasuwa.

D'ago kanta tayi cikin nutsuwa tana kallon zagayayye gashin bakinshi, wanda ya hadu da y'ar gemunshi, sai yayi mata kama da (Shakti arora, wato Dr Kunal na cikin sarkar Zaujee??) shima tsura mata ido yayi amma shi Aishansa ce yake hangowa, gani yake itace a jikinshi, kifa kanshi yayi a fuskarta, tare da tallabo fuskar yana kallon cikin kwayar idanunta, yarrr tsigar jikinta ya mike ta mik'e firgit daga jikinshi ta shiga ha?'a kayanta tabar gurin sai da takai bakin kofa ta juya ta kalleshi ka?an, suka had'a ido murmushi tayi masa tare da juyawa da sauri tabar gurin cike da kunya.

Wato Fatima,
Yara sun sami kyauta ba adadin musaman Iyayena, sunyi min kayan goyo sosai, haka ma Abban Ya Maheer har da fili yabawa takwaransa Abban ya najib kuwa mota sabuwa yabawa Takwaranshi, wanda ake kira da Khalil, macen ana kiran ta Da Hilal.
